### Q101. JEE Main 2012 (07 May Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

In an $L C R$ circuit shown in the following figure, what will be the readings of the voltmeter across the resistor and ammeter if an a.c. source of $220 \mathrm{~V}$ and $100 \mathrm{~Hz}$ is connected to it as shown?
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/5y3szyU4LeOVQIVIx316mYER5fJgcnaV1oxS3xC6F8E.original.fullsize.png">

**Options:**

- **(A)** $800 \mathrm{~V}, 8 \mathrm{~A}$
- **(B)** $110 \mathrm{~V}, 1.1 \mathrm{~A}$
- **(C)** $300 \mathrm{~V}, 3 \mathrm{~A}$
- **(D)** $220 \mathrm{~V}, 2.2 \mathrm{~A}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $220 \mathrm{~V}, 2.2 \mathrm{~A}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In case of series RLC circuit, Equation of voltage is given by
$$
\mathrm{V}^2=V_R^2+\left(V_L-V_C^2\right)
$$
Here, $V=220 V ; V_L=V_C=300 \mathrm{~V}$
$$
\therefore V_R=\sqrt{V^2}=220 \mathrm{~V}
$$
Current $i=\frac{V}{R}=\frac{220}{100}=2.2 \mathrm{~A}$

</details>

### Q102. JEE Main 2011

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A fully charged capacitor $C$ with initial charge $q_0$ is connected to a coil of self inductance $L$ at $t=0$. The time at which the energy is stored equally between the electric and the magnetic field is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{\pi}{4} \sqrt{L C}$
- **(B)** $2 \pi \sqrt{L C}$
- **(C)** $\sqrt{L C}$
- **(D)** $\pi \sqrt{L C}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $\frac{\pi}{4} \sqrt{L C}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Charge oscillates simple harmonic motion $q=q_0 \sin \omega t, U=\frac{1}{2} \frac{q^2}{C}$
$$
\begin{aligned}
& q=\frac{q_0}{\sqrt{2}} \Rightarrow \omega t=\frac{\pi}{4} \\
& \Rightarrow t=\frac{T}{8}=\frac{2 \pi}{8} \sqrt{L C}=\frac{\pi}{4} \sqrt{L C}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q103. JEE Main 2010

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

In a series LCR circuit $R=200 \Omega$ and the voltage and the frequency of the main supply is $220 \mathrm{~V}$ and $50 \mathrm{~Hz}$ respectively. On taking out the capacitance from the circuit the current lags behind the voltage by $30^{\circ}$. On taking out the inductor from the circuit the current leads the voltage by $30^{\circ}$. The power dissipated in the LCR circuit is

**Options:**

- **(A)** $305 \mathrm{~W}$
- **(B)** $210 \mathrm{~W}$
- **(C)** Zero W
- **(D)** $242 \mathrm{~W}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $242 \mathrm{~W}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given circuit is under resonance as $X_L=X_C$ Hence power dissipated in the circuit is
$$
\mathrm{P}=\frac{\mathrm{V}^2}{\mathrm{R}}=242 \mathrm{~W}
$$

</details>

### Q104. JEE Main 2006

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An inductor $(L=100 \mathrm{mH})$, a resistor $(R=100 \Omega)$ and a battery $(E=100 \mathrm{~V})$ are initially connected in series as shown in the figure. After a long time the battery is disconnected after short circuiting the points $A$ and $B$. The current in the circuit $1 \mathrm{~mm}$ after the circuit is
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/FQn0T060PhgUkuEXuQitV5Y2t_zBXcXiPLODOPc-DRk.original.fullsize.png">

**Options:**

- **(A)** $1 \mathrm{~A}$
- **(B)** $1 / \mathrm{e} \mathrm{A}$
- **(C)** $e \mathrm{~A}$
- **(D)** $0.1 \mathrm{~A}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $1 / \mathrm{e} \mathrm{A}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

</details>

### Q105. JEE Main 2006

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

In a series resonant LCR circuit, the voltage across $R$ is 100 volts and $R=1 \mathrm{k} \Omega$ with $C=2 \mu \mathrm{F}$. The resonant frequency $\omega$ is $200 \mathrm{rad} / \mathrm{s}$. At resonance the voltage across $L$ is

**Options:**

- **(A)** $4 \times 10^{-3} \mathrm{~V}$
- **(B)** $2.5 \times 10^{-2} \mathrm{~V}$
- **(C)** $40 \mathrm{~V}$
- **(D)** $250 \mathrm{~V}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $250 \mathrm{~V}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{i}=\frac{100}{1000}=0.1 \mathrm{~A}$
$V_L=V_C=\frac{0.1}{200 \times 2 \times 10^{-6}}=250 \mathrm{~V}$

</details>

The phase difference between the alternating current and emf is $\pi / 2$. Which of the following cannot be the constituent of the circuit?

- **(A)** C alone
- **(B)** R.L
- **(C)** L.C
- **(D)** L alone

### Q107. JEE Main 2004

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a LCR circuit capacitance is changed from $C$ to $2 C$. For the resonant frequency to remain unchanged, the inductance should be changed from $L$ to

**Options:**

- **(A)** $4 \mathrm{~L}$
- **(B)** $2 \mathrm{~L}$
- **(C)** L/2
- **(D)** L/4

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) L/2**

**Detailed Solution:**

$$
\omega_{\text {res }}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{L C}}
$$
if $\omega_{\text {res }}$ is to remain same, the product LC should also not change.
$$
\begin{aligned}
& \Rightarrow \mathrm{LC}=\mathrm{L}^{\prime} \mathrm{C}^{\prime} \\
& \Rightarrow \mathrm{LC}=\mathrm{L}^{\prime} 2 \mathrm{C} \\
& \Rightarrow \mathrm{L}^{\prime}=\mathrm{L}^2 2
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q108. JEE Main 2004

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

In an LCR series a.c. circuit, the voltage across each of the components, $L, C$ and $R$ is $50 \mathrm{~V}$. The voltage across the LC combination will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** $50 \mathrm{~V}$
- **(B)** $50 \sqrt{2} \mathrm{~V}$
- **(C)** $100 \mathrm{~V}$
- **(D)** $0 \mathrm{~V}$ (zero)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $0 \mathrm{~V}$ (zero)**

**Detailed Solution:**

In series LCR circuit, the voltage across the inductor $(L)$ and the capacitor $(C)$ are in opposite phase.

</details>

- **(C)** $\frac{Q}{\sqrt{2}}$
